   82  ***AT SELECTION-SCREEN .

   84  ***  SELECT SINGLE MAX( aznum ) FROM febko INTO aznum
   85  ***                             WHERE bukrs = bukrs
   86  ***                             AND hktid = hktid.
   87  ***  IF sy-subrc = 0.
   88  ***    aznum = aznum + 1 .
   89  ***  ELSE.
   90  ***    CLEAR aznum.
   91  ***  ENDIF.
   92  **** selection logic for bk_actno and cl_actno changed on 160301 due
   93  **** to change in house bank master customizing. House bank master conta
       ins
   94  **** main bank account number and not clearing account number as earlier
       .
   95  ***  SELECT SINGLE hkont FROM t012k INTO bk_actno
   96  ***                      WHERE bukrs = bukrs
   97  ***                      AND   hbkid = hbkid
   98  ***                      AND   hktid = hktid.
   99  ***  IF sy-subrc = 0.

  103  ***        input  = cl_actno
  104  ***      IMPORTING
  105  ***        output = cl_actno.

  321  ***FORM upload_file.
  322  ****begin of <RD1K960036>
  323  **** ----- Upload brs input file for the current month ---- *
  324  ****  CALL FUNCTION 'UPLOAD'
  325  ****       EXPORTING
  326  ****            filename            = 'C:\brs\input\'
  327  ****            filetype            = 'DAT'
  328  ****            item                = 'Read File for BRS'
  329  ****       TABLES
  330  ****            data_tab            = input
  331  ****       EXCEPTIONS
  332  ****            conversion_error    = 1
  333  ****            invalid_table_width = 2
  334  ****            invalid_type        = 3.
  335  ****
  336  ****  IF sy-subrc <> 0.
  337  ****    WRITE:/ 'Unable to open dataset'.
  338  ****
  339  ****  ENDIF.
  340  ***  DATA : i_file_table TYPE  TABLE OF file_table,
  341  ***         l_filetable  TYPE  file_table,
  342  ***         l_rc         TYPE  i,
  343  ***         l_p_def_file TYPE  string,
  344  ***         l_p_file     TYPE  string,
  345  ***         l_usr_act    TYPE  i,
  346  ***         l_title      TYPE string.
  347  ***
  348  ***  l_title = 'Read File for BRS'.
  349  ***  l_p_def_file = 'C:\brs\input\'.
  350  ***  CALL METHOD cl_gui_frontend_services=>file_open_dialog
  351  ***    EXPORTING
  352  ***      window_title            = l_title
  353  ***      initial_directory       = l_p_def_file
  354  ***    CHANGING
  355  ***      file_table              = i_file_table
  356  ***      rc                      = l_rc
  357  ***      user_action             = l_usr_act
  358  ***    EXCEPTIONS
  359  ***      file_open_dialog_failed = 1
  360  ***      cntl_error              = 2
  361  ***      error_no_gui            = 3
  362  ***      not_supported_by_gui    = 4
  363  ***      OTHERS                  = 5.
  364  ***
  365  ***  IF sy-subrc = 0
  366  ***        AND l_usr_act <>
  367  ***        cl_gui_frontend_services=>action_cancel.
  368  ***
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page      9
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  369  ***
  370  ***    LOOP AT i_file_table  INTO l_filetable.
  371  ***      l_p_file = l_filetable.
  372  ***      EXIT.
  373  ***    ENDLOOP.
  374  ***
  375  ***
  376  ***    CALL FUNCTION 'GUI_UPLOAD'
  377  ***      EXPORTING
  378  ***        filename                = l_p_file

  406  ***  ENDIF.

  456  ***  DESCRIBE TABLE input1 LINES line.
  457  ***
  458  ***  IF line <= 1.
  459  ***    text = text-037.
  460  ***    CONCATENATE space text   INTO errtxt SEPARATED BY separator.
  461  ***    PERFORM append_generic_err TABLES err_vald USING 'W' '0'.
  462  ***  ELSEIF line > 1.
  463  ***    aznum1 = aznum - 1.
  464  ***    CONCATENATE bukrs aznum1  hbkid hktid  INTO param_txt1.
  465  ***    READ TABLE input1 INDEX 1 .
  466  ***    CONDENSE input1-narration.
  467  ***    CONDENSE param_txt1.
  468  ***    IF input1-narration = param_txt1.
  469  **** ---- Merge previous month brs input into the current month input --
       -
  470  ***
  471  ***      APPEND LINES OF input1 FROM 2 TO input.
  472  ***
  473  ***    ELSE.
  474  ***      text = text-044.
  475  ***      CONCATENATE space text   INTO errtxt SEPARATED BY separator.
  476  ***      PERFORM append_generic_err TABLES err_vald USING 'W' '0'.
  477  ***    ENDIF.
  478  ***  ENDIF.
  479  ***
  480  ***
  481  **** ----- derive trancode ,chkno,RT# from and store into intab. ---- *
  482  ***  LOOP AT input.
  483  ***    intab-slno =    input-slno.
  484  ***    intab-actno = input-actno.
  485  ***    intab-valdt = input-valdt.
  486  ***    intab-tran_dt = input-tran_dt.
  487  ***    intab-tran_amt = input-tran_amt.
  488  ***    intab-dr_cr =   input-dr_cr.
  489  ***    intab-cum_bal = input-cum_bal.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page     11
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

  490  ***    intab-dr_cr1 =  input-dr_cr1.
  491  ***    intab-narration = input-narration.
  492  ***    intab-errrec = '0'.
  493  ***    TRANSLATE intab-narration TO UPPER CASE.
  494  ***    TRANSLATE intab-dr_cr TO UPPER CASE.
  495  ***    TRANSLATE intab-dr_cr1 TO UPPER CASE.
  496  ***    CONDENSE intab-tran_amt.
  497  ***    CONDENSE intab-cum_bal.
  498  ***    APPEND intab.
  499  ***  ENDLOOP.

  501  ***  PERFORM get_opentab.

  506  ***FORM format_generic_error_check.
  507  ***  cum_bal =  opbl * 100.
  508  ***  LOOP AT intab FROM 2.
  509  ***
  510  ***    reccnt = sy-tabix.
  511  ***    flg_match = 'N'.
  512  ***    flg_dupl = 'N'.
  513  ***    tot_err_flg = 0.
  514  ***    err_flag_rec = '0'.
  515  ***    err_narr = 'N'.
  516  ***    counter = counter + 1.
  517  ***    IF intab-slno(1) <> 'P' AND slno(1) <> 'P'.
  518  ***      diff = intab-slno - slno.
  519  ***      CONDENSE diff.
  520  ***    ENDIF.
  521  **** --- Check if Control no. contains value other than numbers  --- *
  522  ***    IF NOT ( intab-slno CO '0123456789P ').
  523  ****   concatenate intab-slno sy-vline  into errtxt separated by separat
       or.
  524  ***      err_vald-slno = intab-slno.
  525  ***      errtxt = text-003 .

 1789  ***FORM dumping_tofilesystem.
 1790  **** ---- Fill the first record of bankstmt with all fields blank and
 1791  ****------description with the parameter values as file idetifier
 1792  ***  intab-slno = space.
 1793  ***  intab-actno = space.
 1794  ***  intab-valdt = space.
 1795  ***  intab-tran_dt = space.
 1796  ***  intab-narration = space.

 2045  ***FORM derive_other_fields.
 2046  ***  CONDENSE bk_actno.
 2047  ***
 2048  **** ---- derive the last char of bank account no ---- *
 2049  ***  IF cl_actno = '0000091220'.
 2050  ***    tempchar = 'A'.
 2051  ***  ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091230'.
 2052  ***    tempchar = 'B'.
 2053  ***  ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091240'.
 2054  ***    tempchar = 'C'.
 2055  ***  ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091250'.
 2056  ***    tempchar = 'D'.
 2057  ***  ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091260'.
 2058  ***    tempchar = 'E'.
 2059  ***  ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091270'.
 2060  ***    tempchar = 'F'.
 2061  ***  ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091280'.
 2062  ***    tempchar = 'G'.
 2063  ***  ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091290'.
 2064  ***    tempchar = 'H'.
 2065  ***  ELSE.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page     40
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 2066  ***    len2 = strlen( bk_actno ).
 2067  ***    len2 = len2 - 1.
 2068  ***    tempchar = bk_actno+len2(1).   " The last char of the bank accoun
       tno
 2069  ***  ENDIF.
 2070  ***  CLEAR intab.
 2071  ***
 2072  ***  LOOP AT intab  FROM 2.
 2073  ***
 2074  ***    tot_err_flg = intab-errrec.
 2075  **** ---- if narration length is 8 and it has a format 99999999,tcode=CH
       K*-
 2076  ***    IF  intab-narration CO '0123456789 ' .
 2077  ***      intab-chkno = intab-narration.
 2078  ***      CONCATENATE 'CHK' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 2079  **** ---- if narration length is 7 and it has a format R999999,tcode=RCT
       * -
 2080  ***    ELSEIF intab-narration(1) = 'R' OR intab-narration(1) = 'C' AND
 2081  ***        intab-narration+1(6) CO '1234567890 '  .
 2082  ***      intab-rt# = intab-narration.
 2083  ***      CONCATENATE 'RCT' tempchar INTO  intab-tcode.
 2084  **** ---- if narration length is 7 and it has a format D999999,tcode=OTD
       * -
 2085  ***    ELSEIF intab-narration(1) = 'D' AND
 2086  ***        intab-narration+1(6) CO '1234567890 '  .
 2087  ***      intab-rt# = intab-narration.
 2088  ***      CONCATENATE 'OTD' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 2089  **** - if narration is 'BCH' or 'INT' or 'INA' then tcode is same as nar
       rt
 2090  ***    ELSEIF intab-narration = 'BCH'.
 2091  ***      CONCATENATE 'BCH' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 2092  ***    ELSEIF intab-narration = 'INT'.
 2093  ***      CONCATENATE 'INT' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 2094  ***    ELSEIF intab-narration = 'INC'.
 2095  ***      CONCATENATE 'INC' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 2096  ***    ELSEIF intab-narration = 'SI'.
 2097  ***      intab-tcode = 'TRAN'.
 2098  ****Included for SCA -kameswari
 2099  ***    ELSEIF intab-narration = 'SCA'.
 2100  ***      intab-tcode = 'TRAN'.
 2101  ****Included for SCA -kameswari
 2102  ***    ENDIF.

 2120  **** - Identify whether there is a matching check in the bank book---*
 2121  ***      READ TABLE opentab WITH KEY chect = intab-chkno.
 2122  ***      IF sy-subrc = 0  .
 2123  ***********************
 2124  ***        intab-gsber = opentab-gsber.
 2125  ***********************

 2129  ***          rwbtr = intab-tran_amt / 100.
 2130  ***          IF intab-dr_cr = 'D' .
 2131  ***            rwbtr = 0 - rwbtr.
 2132  ***          ENDIF.
 2133  ***          IF opentab-shkzg = 'H'.
 2134  ***            wrbtr1 = 0 - opentab-dmbtr.
 2135  ***          ELSE.
 2136  ***            wrbtr1 = opentab-dmbtr.
 2137  ***          ENDIF.
 2138  ****---- Transaction Amt should be same as Check Amt in Payr Table ---*
 2139  ***          IF wrbtr1 <> rwbtr.
 2140  ***            tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 2141  ***            new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 2142  ***            PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-chkno
 2143  ***             intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 2144  ***             opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg '' '' ''.
 2145  ***          ENDIF.
 2146  ***        ENDIF.
 2147  **** ---- Check is encashed ---- *
 2148  ***        IF NOT ( opentab-bancd  = '00000000' ).
 2149  ***

 2151  ***          IF sy-subrc = 0.
 2152  ***            match_tab-encash = 'Y'.

 2154  ***          ELSE.

 2158  ***            opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg '' 'Y' ''.
 2159  ***          ENDIF.
 2160  ***        ENDIF.

 3596  ***FORM get_unmatched_forex_data.
 3597  ***  SELECT SINGLE hkont FROM t012k INTO bk_actno
 3598  ***                      WHERE bukrs = bukrs
 3599  ***                      AND   hbkid = hbkid
 3600  ***                      AND   hktid = hktid.
 3601  ***  IF sy-subrc = 0.
 3602  ***    cl_actno = bk_actno + 2.
 3603  ***    CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'
 3604  ***      EXPORTING
 3605  ***        input  = cl_actno
 3606  ***      IMPORTING
 3607  ***        output = cl_actno.
 3608  ****    temp_bukrs = bukrs.
 3609  ****    CONCATENATE hktid temp_bukrs aznum+1(4) INTO session.
 3610  ****  ELSE.
 3611  ****    CLEAR session.
 3612  ***  ENDIF.
 3613  ***  gjahr1 = gjahr - 1.
 3614  ***  CLEAR l_tot_rs_amt.
 3615  ***  SELECT
 3616  ***    augbl zuonr gjahr belnr
 3617  ***    budat blart shkzg gsber
 3618  ***    dmbtr sgtxt waers wrbtr  INTO
 3619  ***    (bsis-augbl, bsis-zuonr, bsis-gjahr, bsis-belnr,
 3620  ***    bsis-budat, bsis-blart, bsis-shkzg, bsis-gsber,
 3621  ***    bsis-dmbtr, bsis-sgtxt, bsis-waers, bsis-wrbtr )
 3622  ***    FROM bsis
 3623  ***         WHERE bukrs = bukrs AND
 3624  ***               hkont = cl_actno AND
 3625  ***               augbl = ' ' AND
 3626  ***               ( gjahr = gjahr OR gjahr = gjahr1 ) AND
 3627  ***               budat <= dt_to .
 3628  ***    APPEND bsis TO ist_forex.
 3629  ***    CASE bsis-shkzg.
 3630  ***      WHEN 'S'.
 3631  ***        l_tot_rs_amt = l_tot_rs_amt + bsis-dmbtr.
 3632  ***      WHEN 'H'.
 3633  ***        l_tot_rs_amt = l_tot_rs_amt - bsis-dmbtr.
 3634  ***    ENDCASE.
 3635  ***  ENDSELECT.
 3636  ***
 3637  ***ENDFORM.                    " get_unmatched_forex_data

 5062     WHERE  bukrs =  bukrs  AND
 5063            hkont = bk_actno AND
 5064            gjahr = gjahr AND
 5065            blart = 'CC'  AND
 5066            budat < dt_fm AND
 5067            shkzg = 'H'.

 5071    WHERE  bukrs =  bukrs  AND
 5072           hkont = bk_actno AND
 5073           gjahr = gjahr AND
 5074           blart = 'CC'  AND
 5075           budat < dt_fm AND
 5076           shkzg = 'S'.
 5077    amount_5 = amount_4 - amount_1.

 5579  FORM append_match USING new_seq slno chkno tran_amt dr_cr

 5854  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 5855  *&      Form  DERIVE_OTHER_FIELDS
 5856  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 5857  FORM derive_other_fields.
 5858    CONDENSE bk_actno.
 5859
 5860  * ---- derive the last char of bank account no ---- *
 5861    IF cl_actno = '0000091220'.
 5862      tempchar = 'A'.
 5863    ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091230'.
 5864      tempchar = 'B'.
 5865    ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091240'.
 5866      tempchar = 'C'.
 5867    ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091250'.
 5868      tempchar = 'D'.
 5869    ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091260'.
 5870      tempchar = 'E'.
 5871    ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091270'.
 5872      tempchar = 'F'.
 5873    ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091280'.
 5874      tempchar = 'G'.
 5875    ELSEIF cl_actno = '0000091290'.
 5876      tempchar = 'H'.
 5877    ELSE.
 5878      len2 = strlen( bk_actno ).
 5879      len2 = len2 - 1.
 5880      tempchar = bk_actno+len2(1).   " The last char of the bank accountno
 5881    ENDIF.
 5882    CLEAR intab.
 5883
 5884    LOOP AT intab  FROM 2.
 5885
 5886      tot_err_flg = intab-errrec.
 5887  * ---- if narration length is 8 and it has a format 99999999,tcode=CHK*-
 5888      IF  intab-narration CO '0123456789 ' .
 5889        intab-chkno = intab-narration.
 5890        CONCATENATE 'CHK' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 5891  * ---- if narration length is 7 and it has a format R999999,tcode=RCT* -
 5892      ELSEIF intab-narration(1) = 'R' OR intab-narration(1) = 'C' AND
 5893          intab-narration+1(6) CO '1234567890 '  .
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page    107
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 5894        intab-rt# = intab-narration.
 5895        CONCATENATE 'RCT' tempchar INTO  intab-tcode.
 5896  * ---- if narration length is 7 and it has a format D999999,tcode=OTD* -
 5897      ELSEIF intab-narration(1) = 'D' AND
 5898          intab-narration+1(6) CO '1234567890 '  .
 5899        intab-rt# = intab-narration.
 5900        CONCATENATE 'OTD' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 5901  * - if narration is 'BCH' or 'INT' or 'INA' then tcode is same as narrt
 5902      ELSEIF intab-narration = 'BCH'.
 5903        CONCATENATE 'BCH' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 5904      ELSEIF intab-narration = 'INT'.
 5905        CONCATENATE 'INT' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 5906      ELSEIF intab-narration = 'INC'.
 5907        CONCATENATE 'INC' tempchar INTO intab-tcode.
 5908      ELSEIF intab-narration = 'SI'.
 5909        intab-tcode = 'TRAN'.
 5910  *Included for SCA -kameswari
 5911      ELSEIF intab-narration = 'SCA'.
 5912        intab-tcode = 'TRAN'.
 5913  *Included for SCA -kameswari
 5914      ENDIF.
 5915      intab-errrec = tot_err_flg.
 5916      MODIFY intab FROM intab INDEX sy-tabix.
 5917    ENDLOOP.
 5918  ENDFORM.                               " DERIVE_OTHER_FIELDS
 5919
 5920  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 5921  *&      Form  VALIDATION_ERROR_CHECK
 5922  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 5923  FORM validation_error_check.
 5924    LOOP AT intab FROM 2.
 5925      reccnt = sy-tabix.
 5926      flg_match = 'N'.
 5927      rt_count = 0.pre_count = 0. rwbtr = 0.
 5928      tot_err_flg = intab-errrec.
 5929  ***************** The Matching Starts here ***********************
 5930  * ---- If there is a Check No ------ *
 5931      IF NOT ( intab-chkno IS INITIAL ).
 5932  * - Identify whether there is a matching check in the bank book---*
 5933        READ TABLE opentab WITH KEY chect = intab-chkno.
 5934        IF sy-subrc = 0  .
 5935  ********************
 5936          intab-gsber = opentab-gsber.
 5937  ********************
 5938          posn_opentab = sy-tabix.
 5939          flg_match = 'Y'.
 5940          IF  intab-tran_amt CO '1234567890 '.
 5941            rwbtr = intab-tran_amt / 100.
 5942            IF intab-dr_cr = 'D' .
 5943              rwbtr = 0 - rwbtr.
 5944            ENDIF.
 5945            IF opentab-shkzg = 'H'.
 5946              wrbtr1 = 0 - opentab-dmbtr.
 5947            ELSE.
 5948              wrbtr1 = opentab-dmbtr.
 5949            ENDIF.
 5950  *---- Transaction Amt should be same as Check Amt in Payr Table ---*
 5951            IF wrbtr1 <> rwbtr.
 5952              tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 5953              new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 5954              PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-chkno
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page    108
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 5955               intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 5956               opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg '' '' ''.
 5957            ENDIF.
 5958          ENDIF.
 5959  * ---- Check is encashed ---- *
 5960          IF NOT ( opentab-bancd  = '00000000' ).
 5961
 5962            READ TABLE match_tab WITH KEY identif = intab-chkno.
 5963            IF sy-subrc = 0.
 5964              match_tab-encash = 'Y'.
 5965              MODIFY match_tab INDEX sy-tabix.
 5966            ELSE.
 5967              new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 5968              PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-chkno
 5969              intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 5970              opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg '' 'Y' ''.
 5971            ENDIF.
 5972          ENDIF.
 5973
 5974  *--- If it is not in Bankbook table(Payr) , identify if there is a
 5975  *--- Matching entry for the checkno
 5976  * in the allocation field of any doc-*
 5977        ELSEIF sy-subrc <> 0.
 5978          LOOP AT opentab WHERE zuonr = intab-chkno.
 5979            pre_count = pre_count + 1.
 5980          ENDLOOP.
 5981          IF pre_count > 1.
 5982            new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 5983            tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 5984
 5985            LOOP AT opentab WHERE zuonr = intab-chkno .
 5986
 5987              PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-chkno
 5988               intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 5989           opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg space space space.
 5990            ENDLOOP.
 5991
 5992          ELSEIF pre_count = 1.
 5993
 5994
 5995            READ TABLE opentab WITH KEY zuonr = intab-chkno .
 5996            IF sy-subrc = 0.
 5997              intab-docno = opentab-belnr.
 5998              intab-gsber = opentab-gsber.
 5999              flg_match = 'Y'.
 6000
 6001              posn_opentab = sy-tabix.
 6002              IF  intab-tran_amt CO '1234567890 '.
 6003                rwbtr = intab-tran_amt / 100.
 6004                IF intab-dr_cr = 'D' .
 6005                  rwbtr = 0 - rwbtr.
 6006                ENDIF.
 6007                IF opentab-shkzg = 'H'.
 6008                  wrbtr1 = 0 - opentab-dmbtr.
 6009                ELSE.
 6010                  wrbtr1 = opentab-dmbtr.
 6011                ENDIF.
 6012  *---- Transaction Amt should be same as Check Amt in Payr Table ---*
 6013                IF wrbtr1 <> rwbtr.
 6014                  tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 6015                  new_seq = new_seq + 1.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page    109
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 6016                  PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-chkn
       o
 6017                             intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 6018                         opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg '' '' '
       '.
 6019                ENDIF.
 6020              ENDIF.
 6021  *-- it is a pre-golive chq the tcode has to be chaged to 'OTD*'----*
 6022              CONCATENATE 'OTD' tempchar INTO tcode.
 6023              intab-tcode = tcode.
 6024              intab-gsber = opentab-gsber.
 6025              MODIFY intab INDEX reccnt.
 6026            ENDIF.
 6027          ENDIF.
 6028          IF flg_match <> 'Y' AND pre_count = 0.
 6029  * - Check no not in Bank book(Payr) nor in the alloc. field of a doc.-*
 6030            tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 6031            new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 6032            PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-chkno
 6033           intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 6034           space space space space space space.
 6035          ENDIF.
 6036        ENDIF.
 6037
 6038
 6039  * --- Check for RT# ----- *
 6040
 6041      ELSEIF NOT ( intab-rt# IS INITIAL ).
 6042
 6043  ***  start<RD1K9A0B6B>
 6044        DATA: str1      TYPE string,
 6045              chr20(20) TYPE c.
 6046        READ TABLE opentab WITH KEY zuonr = intab-rt#.
 6047        IF sy-subrc = 0.
 6048          IF opentab-chect IS NOT INITIAL.
 6049            CLEAR: str1, chr20.
 6050  ***S/4 Start of Change - SAP_ABAP5 — TR RP1K951283   – 2022/05/27
 6051  *          chr20 = opentab-dmbtr .
 6052            chr20 =  CONV #( opentab-dmbtr ).
 6053  ***S/4 End of Change - SAP_ABAP5— TR RP1K951283  – 2022/05/27
 6054            CONDENSE chr20.
 6055            CONCATENATE 'Against bank statement debit for' chr20
 6056                        ',pls use narration chk no.' opentab-chect
 6057                        'assigned with' opentab-belnr INTO str1 SEPARATED
       BY space.
 6058            " error msg to be displayed
 6059            MESSAGE  str1 TYPE 'I'.
 6060
 6061            tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 6062            new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 6063
 6064            LOOP AT opentab WHERE zuonr = intab-rt# .
 6065  *          clear: str1.
 6066  *          CONCATENATE 'use' opentab-CHECT 'in BS narration' into str1.
 6067              PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-rt#
 6068              intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 6069          opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg space space space.
 6070              EXIT.
 6071            ENDLOOP.
 6072
 6073  *          leave to screen 0.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 6074          ENDIF.
 6075        ENDIF.
 6076  ***  end
 6077  *--- The RT# should be there in the allocation field of only one doc--*
 6078  *read table opentab with key zuonr = intab-docno.
 6079        LOOP AT opentab WHERE zuonr = intab-rt#.
 6080          rt_count = rt_count + 1.
 6081        ENDLOOP.
 6082        IF rt_count > 1.
 6083          tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 6084          new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 6085
 6086          LOOP AT opentab WHERE zuonr = intab-rt# .
 6087
 6088            PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-rt#
 6089            intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 6090        opentab-belnr opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg space space space.
 6091          ENDLOOP.
 6092
 6093        ELSEIF rt_count = 0.
 6094          tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 6095          new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 6096          PERFORM append_match USING new_seq intab-slno intab-rt#
 6097         intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr
 6098         space space space space space space.
 6099
 6100        ELSEIF rt_count = 1.
 6101  *--- The amount for the above document should be same as the amt of the
 6102  * --- RT# document.
 6103          READ TABLE opentab WITH KEY zuonr = intab-rt# .
 6104          intab-docno = opentab-belnr.
 6105          intab-gsber = opentab-gsber.
 6106          posn_opentab = sy-tabix.
 6107          flg_match = 'Y'.
 6108          IF intab-tran_amt CO '1234567890 '.
 6109            wrbtr = intab-tran_amt / 100.
 6110            IF intab-dr_cr = 'D'.
 6111              wrbtr = 0 - wrbtr.
 6112            ENDIF.
 6113            IF opentab-shkzg = 'H'.
 6114              wrbtr1 = 0 - opentab-dmbtr.
 6115            ELSE.
 6116              wrbtr1 = opentab-dmbtr.
 6117            ENDIF.
 6118            IF wrbtr1 <> wrbtr .
 6119              tot_err_flg = tot_err_flg + 1.
 6120              new_seq = new_seq + 1.
 6121              PERFORM append_match
 6122              USING new_seq intab-slno intab-rt#
 6123              intab-tran_amt intab-dr_cr opentab-belnr
 6124              opentab-dmbtr opentab-shkzg space space space.
 6125            ENDIF.
 6126          ENDIF.
 6127        ENDIF.
 6128      ENDIF.
 6129
 6130      intab-errrec = tot_err_flg.
 6131      MODIFY intab FROM intab INDEX reccnt.
 6132      IF flg_match = 'Y'.
 6133        opentab-errrec = tot_err_flg.
 6134        MODIFY opentab INDEX posn_opentab.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page    111
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 6135      ENDIF.
 6136
 6137    ENDLOOP.
 6138  ENDFORM.                               " VALIDATION_ERROR_CHECK

 6899  FORM upload_bdc_program.
 6900  * -The unmatched records are downloaded to File system as Prev month err
 6901    PERFORM dumping_bnkstmt_err.

 6904      line = line + 1.
 6905    ENDLOOP.
 6906    IF line > 0.
 6907  * --- Calculate Closing Bal.------*
 6908      PERFORM calculate_opbal.
 6909
 6910      PERFORM  generate_header_data.
 6911  * ----- Generate BDCTAB only for matched records ----*
 6912      LOOP AT intab FROM 2 WHERE errrec = '0' AND tcode <> 'TRAN'.
 6913        PERFORM generate_bdc_data.
 6914      ENDLOOP.
 6915  * --- BDC Call transaction and the BDC Lod to be downloed to file system
 6916      PERFORM bdc_insert.
 6917    ELSE.
 6918      WRITE:/ 'There is no matching record to be uploaded...'.
 6919  *begin of <RD1K960036>
 6920  *WRITE:/'The Unmatched Records have been downloaded to
 6921  *c:\brs\prevmonth.txt'.
 6922      WRITE:/'The Unmatched Records have been downloaded to'
 6923      &'c:\brs\prevmonth.txt'.
 6924  *end of <RD1K960036>
 6925    ENDIF.

 7004  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7005  *&      Form  GENERATE_BDC_DATA
 7006  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7007  FORM generate_bdc_data.
 7008  *added for removing inconsistency of screen no in DEV and PRD -
 7009  *for variant BANK kameswari
 7010    CLEAR t021d.
 7011    SELECT SINGLE * FROM t021d WHERE progn = 'SAPMF40K'
 7012                                 AND anwnd = 'KNTAZ'
 7013                                 AND varnr = 'BANK'.
 7014
 7015    PERFORM bdc_dynpro      USING 'SAPMF40K' t021d-dynnr.
 7016  *added for removing inconsistency of screen no in DEV and PRD -
 7017  *for variant BANK kameswari
 7018    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'BDC_OKCODE' 'ZINS'.
 7019    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'BDC_CURSOR' 'FEBMKA-VGMAN(01)'."Transac
 7020
 7021    PERFORM bdc_field       USING  'FEBMKA-VGMAN(01)' intab-tcode.
 7022
 7023  * -- Convert The Date into SAP Date Format ---*
 7024    IF intab-valdt(2) = '99'.
 7025      CONCATENATE '19' intab-valdt INTO tempdt1.
 7026
 7027    ELSE.
 7028  *Commented on 2-2-2001 for Fiscal year - kameswari
 7029  *   IF INTAB-VALDT(2) = '01'.
 7030  *Commented on 2-2-2001 for Fiscal year - kameswari
 7031      CONCATENATE '20' intab-valdt INTO tempdt1.
 7032    ENDIF.
 7033  *concatenate tempdt+4(2) '/' tempdt+6(2) '/' tempdt(4) into tempdt.
 7034    WRITE tempdt1 TO sy-tvar0.
 7035
 7036    PERFORM bdc_field       USING  'FEBEP-VALUT(01)' sy-tvar0. " Value dt
 7037    intab-tran_amt = intab-tran_amt / 100.
 7038    IF intab-dr_cr = 'D'.
 7039      tran_amt = 0 - intab-tran_amt.
 7040    ELSE.
 7041      tran_amt = intab-tran_amt.
 7042    ENDIF.
 7043  ***S/4 Start of Change - SAP_ABAP5 — TR RP1K951283   – 2022/05/27
 7044  *  tran_amt1 = tran_amt.
 7045    tran_amt1 = CONV #( tran_amt ).
 7046  ***S/4 End of Change - SAP_ABAP5— TR RP1K951283  – 2022/05/27
 7047    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'FEBMKA-KWBTR(01)' tran_amt1." Amt
 7048
 7049    IF intab-tcode(3) = 'RCT' OR intab-tcode(3) = 'OTD'.
 7050      PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'FEBMKK-CHECT_KF(01)' intab-docno.
 7051    ELSEIF intab-tcode(3) = 'CHK'.
 7052      PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'FEBMKK-CHECT_KF(01)' intab-chkno.
 7053    ENDIF.
 7054    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'FEBMKK-ZUONR(01)' intab-narration.
 7055    IF intab-tcode(3) = 'BCH' OR intab-tcode(3) = 'INT'
 7056                                          OR intab-tcode(3) = 'INC'.
 7057      PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'FEBMKK-KOSTL_KF(01)' kostl.
 7058    ENDIF.
 7059    CONCATENATE
 7060  'Company:' bukrs ';' 'Stmt No:' aznum ';' 'Slno:' intab-slno INTO descr.
 7061    PERFORM bdc_field USING 'FEBMKK-SGTXT_KF(01)' descr.
 7062    PERFORM bdc_field USING 'FEBMKK-GSBER_KF(01)' intab-gsber.
 7063    IF intab-tcode(3) = 'BCH' OR intab-tcode(3) = 'INT'
 7064                                        OR intab-tcode(3) = 'INC'.
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page    127
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 7065  *Code included for BA as told by Rohit - kameswari
 7066      PERFORM bdc_field USING 'FEBMKK-GSBER_KF(01)' gsber.
 7067  *Code included for BA as told by Rohit - kameswari
 7068    ENDIF.
 7069  * ----
 7070  ENDFORM.                               " GENERATE_BDC_DATA
 7071  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7072  *&      Form  BDC_INSERT
 7073  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7074  FORM bdc_insert.
 7075    PERFORM bdc_dynpro      USING 'SAPMF40K' t021d-dynnr.
 7076    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'BDC_OKCODE' 'SICH'.
 7077    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'BDC_CURSOR' 'FEBMKK-CHECT_KF(01)'.
 7078    PERFORM bdc_dynpro      USING 'SAPMF40K' '0101'.
 7079    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'BDC_OKCODE' 'BUCH'.
 7080    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'BDC_CURSOR' 'FEBMKA-BUKRS'.
 7081    PERFORM bdc_dynpro      USING 'SAPMF40K' '0101'.
 7082    PERFORM bdc_field       USING 'BDC_OKCODE' '/N'.
 7083    CALL TRANSACTION 'FF67' USING bdcdata MODE 'N' UPDATE 'S'
 7084                                          MESSAGES INTO bdcmsg.
 7085    IF sy-subrc = 0.
 7086      WRITE :/ 'The matched items has been uploaded to BDC Session ',sessi
       on.
 7087    ENDIF.
 7088    PERFORM format_message .
 7089    PERFORM display_call_transaction_log.
 7090  ENDFORM.                               " BDC_INSERT
 7091  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7092  *&      Form  CALCULATE_OPBAL
 7093  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7094  FORM calculate_opbal.
 7095    tempbal1 = 0.
 7096    LOOP AT intab FROM 2 WHERE errrec = '0' AND tcode <> 'TRAN'.
 7097      IF intab-dr_cr = 'D'.
 7098        tempbal1 = tempbal1 - intab-tran_amt / 100.
 7099      ELSEIF intab-dr_cr = 'C'.
 7100        tempbal1 = tempbal1 + intab-tran_amt / 100.
 7101      ENDIF.
 7102    ENDLOOP.
 7103
 7104  ENDFORM.                               " CALCULATE_OPBAL

 7231    line = 0.

 7233      line = line + 1.

 7235    IF line > 0.
 7236      aznum1 = aznum.

 7239    ENDIF.

 7263      ENDLOOP.
 7264  * ---- Download the bank stmt error records to file system ----- *
 7265  *    CALL FUNCTION 'WS_DOWNLOAD'

 7351      match_amt = intab-tran_amt.
 7352      match_amt1 = match_amt / 100.
 7353      IF intab-tcode = 'TRAN' AND intab-narration = 'SCA'.
 7354        line = line + 1.

 7356           AND   intab-tran_amt CO '1234567890 '.
 7357          IF intab-dr_cr = 'D'.

 7359          ELSEIF intab-dr_cr = 'C'.
 7360            tot_transc =  tot_transc + intab-tran_amt.
 7361          ENDIF.

       AP3  RP1K951283 27-May-22
 7364  *      WRITE :/3 intab-slno,11 sy-vline,
 7365  *               13 intab-valdt,28 sy-vline,
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 7372        l_amt =  match_amt1.
 7373  *** EOC by SAP on 10/01/2023 To handle increased amount length
 7374

 7381                 59 intab-narration,92 sy-vline.

       3  RP1K951283 27-May-22
 7383      ENDIF.
 7384    ENDLOOP.

 7386  ** Changes made on 12.10.2001

 7503  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7504  *&      Form  get_unmatched_forex_data
 7505  *&---------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7506  *       text
 7507  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7508  *  -->  p1        text
 7509  *  <--  p2        text
 7510  *----------------------------------------------------------------------*
 7511  FORM get_unmatched_forex_data.
 7512    SELECT SINGLE hkont FROM t012k INTO bk_actno
 7513                        WHERE bukrs = bukrs
 7514                        AND   hbkid = hbkid
 7515                        AND   hktid = hktid.
 7516    IF sy-subrc = 0.
 7517      cl_actno = bk_actno + 2.
 7518      CALL FUNCTION 'CONVERSION_EXIT_ALPHA_INPUT'
 7519        EXPORTING
 7520          input  = cl_actno
 7521        IMPORTING
 7522          output = cl_actno.
 7523  *    temp_bukrs = bukrs.
 7524  *    CONCATENATE hktid temp_bukrs aznum+1(4) INTO session.
 7525  *  ELSE.
 7526  *    CLEAR session.
 7527    ENDIF.
 7528    gjahr1 = gjahr - 1.
 7529    CLEAR l_tot_rs_amt.
 7530    SELECT
 7531      augbl zuonr gjahr belnr
 7532      budat blart shkzg gsber
 7533      dmbtr sgtxt waers wrbtr  INTO
 7534      (bsis-augbl, bsis-zuonr, bsis-gjahr, bsis-belnr,
 7535      bsis-budat, bsis-blart, bsis-shkzg, bsis-gsber,
 7536      bsis-dmbtr, bsis-sgtxt, bsis-waers, bsis-wrbtr )
 7537      FROM bsis
 7538           WHERE bukrs = bukrs AND
 7539                 hkont = cl_actno AND
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10.10.26 OCD      Program ZFI_CNB_BRS                           Page    135
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 7540                 augbl = ' ' AND
 7541                 ( gjahr = gjahr OR gjahr = gjahr1 ) AND
 7542                 budat <= dt_to .
 7543      APPEND bsis TO ist_forex.
 7544      CASE bsis-shkzg.
 7545        WHEN 'S'.
 7546          l_tot_rs_amt = l_tot_rs_amt + bsis-dmbtr.
 7547        WHEN 'H'.
 7548          l_tot_rs_amt = l_tot_rs_amt - bsis-dmbtr.
 7549      ENDCASE.
 7550    ENDSELECT.
 7551
 7552  ENDFORM.                    " get_unmatched_forex_data

 7564  *
 7565  *  DATA: disp    TYPE REF TO zcl_basic_functioins,
 7566  *        l_tcode TYPE swo_typeid.
 7567  *
 7568  *  CLEAR : l_tcode.
 7569  *
 7570  *  CREATE OBJECT disp.
 7571  *  l_tcode = sy-tcode.
 7572  *
 7573  *  CALL METHOD disp->display_attachments
 7574  *    EXPORTING
 7575  *      ztxncode   = l_tcode
 7576  *      on_display = 'X'.
 7577  *
 7578  *
 7579  *ENDFORM.

Title:
   Bank Reconciliation Report

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Text elements:

001   Controls
002   Others
003   Control No  not  proper
004   Value date is greater than Statement date
005   Transaction code not valid for Bank Account No
006   Debit/Credit Indicator not Correct
007   The Amount is not same as the  amount in Check
008   Check no not valid
009   Check has been encashed
010   The payment document has been cleared
011   G/L Ac  for check is not same as bank clearing Ac.
013   More than one matching doc. found for RT#
014   The Document no for RT# is not a open item
015   The Doc. Amt for RT# <> transaction amt
016   G/L Account for RT# is not same as bank clearing Account
017   The Sum of all Transaction Amounts Rs. does not add up to the Difference b
018   G/L Ac. for check is blank
019   RT# can not be blank for transaction code RCT*
020   Payment Document for the check is blank
021   Not a valid Narration
022   No matching doc. found for RT#
023   Output Control ( Perform Activities in the Specified Order)
024   BDC Session Name
025   Upload BDC Session
026   Amount not valid hence not included for sum of transactions
027   Duplicate Narration
028   Transaction Date () not valid
029   Transaction date        From :
030   To :
031   Document no not valid(Valid format is R999999/D999999)
032   Value Date () not valid
033   The Document no for pre-go live  Check is not a open item
034   For Chk no match in bankbook or in alloc.field(Pre-go Live Chk)
035   D/C indicator incompatible with narration
036   Cum bal not proper:It should be
037   No previous month records Available
038   Narration Can not be blank
039   Transaction amt can not be blank
040   Cumulative amt can not be blank
041   Validation/Matching Program
042   BRS Rep(Unmatched/Trans/Rev)
043
044   Inconsistent File header in Previous month Error file
045   Save the Reports in Local file and generate final BRS.Pl. Get it approved.
